How to read these scores: “Original-key match” measures recovery of the original passage meaning. A different A–D answer can be faithful to a mistranslated passage. E means the supplied passage does not provide enough information; it is counted separately, without assuming that every omitted passage requires E. No per-question answerability judgments or revised keys are introduced.

Passage extraction and reproducibility

Every question uses its original curated chapter/verse window. Some saved omissions delete verse-number markers along with clauses. For those passages, the exact recorded deleted units are mapped to their original verses; the reconstructed full variant must match the saved variant. Deleted text and verse markers are never restored. This avoids merging adjacent verses or falling back to a larger passage. These omission windows can therefore differ from older variant runs that relied only on surviving verse numbers. Other variants use the existing verse extraction unchanged.

Temperature 0; context 8,192; output limit 2,500 tokens. One fresh conversation per question and condition. Original A–E choices and answer keys unchanged. Exact model digests match the clean run. Direct deterministic scoring, no LLM grader and no random fallback. Rates describe the saved full-passage conditions, not a guaranteed percentage within each local window.
